- New Code (lines 1-31):
```julia
# ## Methodology: Feature-Driven $h$-Refinement
# The baseline AMR sensor is the Löhner curvature indicator of density.
# `ControllerThreeLevel` retains the Run #9 levels 4/5/6 and thresholds,
# while `AMRCallback` adapts the mesh every 20 timesteps.
#
# The optional shear-aware configuration is activated only by the later
# late-time p=5/p=7 filament-difference trigger. It augments the density
# signal with a vertical-velocity shear contribution masked to the mixing
# layer and permits one additional local refinement level, max_level=7.
# Pure light/heavy regions have zero shear-augmentation mask, preventing
# global refinement on velocity. Shock capturing is not changed.
# %%
@inline function mixing_layer_shear_density(u, equations)
    rho = u[1]
    vertical_velocity = u[3] / rho

    # Compact density mask: zero in pure rho=1 or rho=2 fluid and one at
    # rho=1.5. Thus the additional shear signal is restricted to the
    # material transition/mixing layer, including its rolled-up filaments.
    mixing_mask = clamp(4.0 * (rho - 1.0) * (2.0 - rho), 0.0, 1.0)

    # IndicatorLöhner senses curvature of this combined signal. The masked
    # velocity contribution therefore adds shear sensitivity without using
    # velocity as a domain-wide refinement variable.
    return rho + 10.0 * mixing_mask * vertical_velocity
end

function make_amr_callback(run_semi; shear_aware=false)
    variable = density_indicator
    max_level = 6

    if shear_aware
        variable = mixing_layer_shear_density
        max_level = 7
    end

    indicator = IndicatorLöhner(run_semi, variable=variable)
    controller = ControllerThreeLevel(run_semi, indicator,
                                      base_level=4,
                                      med_level=5, med_threshold=0.1,
                                      max_level=max_level,
                                      max_threshold=0.3)
    callback = AMRCallback(run_semi, controller,
                           interval=20,
                           adapt_initial_condition=true)

    # Metadata allow archival and the conditional rerun to record the actual
    # AMR configuration rather than assuming that every run uses max_level=6.
    configuration = (indicator=indicator,
                     controller=controller,
                     shear_aware=shear_aware,
                     max_level=max_level,
                     levels=(4, 5, max_level),
                     interval=20)
    return callback, configuration
end

# Baseline p=7 configuration: identical to Run #9 density AMR with max=6.
amr_callback, amr_configuration = make_amr_callback(semi)
amr_indicator = amr_configuration.indicator
amr_controller = amr_configuration.controller
```

```julia
# Construct otherwise identical callbacks for companion or conditional runs.
# The supplied semidiscretization and AMR callback must belong to that run.
# Defaults describe baseline density AMR; run_id separates a conditional
# targeted archive from the baseline archive at the same polynomial degree.
function make_run_callbacks(run_semi, run_amr_callback, run_polydeg;
                            run_amr_configuration=(shear_aware=false,
                                                   max_level=6,
                                                   levels=(4, 5, 6),
                                                   interval=20),
                            run_id="p$(run_polydeg)")
    run_npz_out = joinpath(npz_root, run_id)
    run_save_npz_callback, run_save_final_npz_callback =
        make_npz_callbacks(run_npz_out, run_polydeg;
                           run_amr_configuration=run_amr_configuration)

    run_trixi_out = joinpath(trixi_root, run_id)
    run_save_solution = SaveSolutionCallback(dt=dt_save,
                                             save_initial_solution=true,
                                             save_final_solution=true,
                                             output_directory=run_trixi_out,
                                             solution_variables=cons2cons)

    return CallbackSet(SummaryCallback(),
                       AnalysisCallback(run_semi,
                                        interval=analysis_interval),
                       AliveCallback(analysis_interval=analysis_interval),
                       run_save_solution,
                       run_amr_callback,
                       run_save_npz_callback,
                       run_save_final_npz_callback,
                       StepsizeCallback())
end
```

```julia
println("Starting p=7 primary RTI run...")
sol = solve(ode, RDPK3SpFSAL49();
            dt=1.0e-5, callback=callbacks, adaptive=false);

# Preserve explicit primary-run handles for certification and post-processing.
polydeg_p7 = polydeg
semi_p7 = semi
ode_p7 = ode
sol_p7 = sol
checks_p7 = log_run_checks("p=7 primary", sol_p7, semi_p7)

# Cell 12 uses the baseline archive for the p=5/p=7 difference trigger.
# If activated, the targeted run is archived separately before becoming the
# primary p=7 dataset for visualization and certification.
npz_out_p7_baseline = npz_out
p7_targeted_run = nothing

npz_out_p5 = joinpath(npz_root, "p5")
npz_out_p3 = joinpath(npz_root, "p3")
p5_archive_reused = complete_npz_archive(npz_out_p5, 5)
p3_archive_available = complete_npz_archive(npz_out_p3, 3)

# Construct and execute an additional run without changing any physics,
# fluxes, shock-capturing parameters, time-step control, or baseline AMR
# settings. Only the polynomial degree and the explicitly requested AMR mode
# vary. This function is also the reusable conditional-rerun pathway.
function run_rti_case(run_polydeg;
                      shear_aware=false,
                      run_id="p$(run_polydeg)",
                      label="p=$(run_polydeg)")
    1 <= run_polydeg <= 7 ||
        error("The polynomial degree must satisfy 1 <= polydeg <= 7.")

    run_basis = LobattoLegendreBasis(run_polydeg)
    run_indicator_sc = IndicatorHennemannGassner(
        equations, run_basis,
        alpha_max=0.5,
        alpha_min=0.001,
        alpha_smooth=true,
        variable=density_indicator)
    run_volume_integral = VolumeIntegralShockCapturingHG(
        run_indicator_sc;
        volume_flux_dg=flux_ranocha,
        volume_flux_fv=flux_lax_friedrichs)
    run_dg = DGSEM(polydeg=run_polydeg,
                   surface_flux=flux_hllc,
                   volume_integral=run_volume_integral)

    run_mesh = P4estMesh((1, 1), polydeg=run_polydeg,
                         coordinates_min=coordinates_min,
                         coordinates_max=coordinates_max,
                         periodicity=(true, false))
    run_semi = SemidiscretizationHyperbolic(
        run_mesh, equations, initial_condition_rti, run_dg;
        boundary_conditions=boundary_conditions,
        source_terms=source_terms_gravity)

    run_amr_callback, run_amr_configuration =
        make_amr_callback(run_semi; shear_aware=shear_aware)
    run_ode = semidiscretize(run_semi, tspan)
    run_callbacks = make_run_callbacks(
        run_semi, run_amr_callback, run_polydeg;
        run_amr_configuration=run_amr_configuration,
        run_id=run_id)

    if shear_aware
        println("Starting ", label,
                " RTI run with mixing-layer shear-aware AMR ",
                "and local max_level=7...")
    else
        println("Starting ", label, " RTI run with baseline density AMR...")
    end

    run_sol = solve(run_ode, RDPK3SpFSAL49();
                    dt=1.0e-5, callback=run_callbacks, adaptive=false)
    run_checks = log_run_checks(label, run_sol, run_semi)

    run_npz_out = joinpath(npz_root, run_id)
    complete_npz_archive(run_npz_out, run_polydeg) ||
        error("The $(label) run did not produce a complete NPZ archive in " *
              run_npz_out)

    return (polydeg=run_polydeg,
            run_id=run_id,
            npz_directory=run_npz_out,
            shear_aware=shear_aware,
            semi=run_semi,
            ode=run_ode,
            sol=run_sol,
            checks=run_checks,
            amr_callback=run_amr_callback,
            amr_configuration=run_amr_configuration)
end

# Conditional pathway invoked only by Cell 12 after its late-time common-grid
# diagnostic establishes that p=5/p=7 differences persist in the filaments.
function run_targeted_p7()
    return run_rti_case(7;
                        shear_aware=true,
                        run_id="p7_shear",
                        label="p=7 shear-aware targeted")
end

# The p=5 companion is run only when a complete Run #9 archive is unavailable.
polydeg_p5 = 5
sol_p5 = nothing
semi_p5 = nothing
ode_p5 = nothing
checks_p5 = nothing

if p5_archive_reused
    println("Reusing complete archived Run #9 p=5 companion snapshots from ",
            npz_out_p5)
else
    p5_run = run_rti_case(polydeg_p5; label="p=5 companion")
    sol_p5 = p5_run.sol
    semi_p5 = p5_run.semi
    ode_p5 = p5_run.ode
    checks_p5 = p5_run.checks
end

if p3_archive_available
    println("Reusing complete archived Run #9 p=3 snapshots from ", npz_out_p3)
else
    println("Complete archived Run #9 p=3 snapshots are unavailable; ",
            "running p=3 once to complete the mandatory certification trend.")
    p3_run = run_rti_case(3; label="p=3 certification")
    p3_archive_available = complete_npz_archive(npz_out_p3, 3)
    p3_archive_available ||
        error("The p=3 certification archive is incomplete; the p=3 trend " *
              "must not be omitted.")
end
```

```julia
# Vorticity is computed only for the conditional AMR diagnostic. It is not
# included in summary_all.png.
function common_grid_vorticity(grid)
    rho = grid.rho
    horizontal_velocity = grid.u[:, :, 2] ./ rho
    vertical_velocity = grid.v
    ny, nx = size(vertical_velocity)
    dvdx = similar(vertical_velocity)
    dudy = similar(horizontal_velocity)

    for j in 1:ny, i in 1:nx
        # Periodic x derivative.
        i_minus = i == 1 ? nx : i - 1
        i_plus = i == nx ? 1 : i + 1
        dvdx[j, i] = (vertical_velocity[j, i_plus] -
                      vertical_velocity[j, i_minus]) / (2.0 * grid.dx)

        # One-sided derivatives at the two wall-adjacent grid rows.
        j_minus = max(j - 1, 1)
        j_plus = min(j + 1, ny)
        dudy[j, i] = (horizontal_velocity[j_plus, i] -
                      horizontal_velocity[j_minus, i]) /
                     ((j_plus - j_minus) * grid.dy)
    end

    return abs.(dvdx .- dudy)
end

function filament_difference_diagnostic(grid_p5, grid_p7)
    rho_l2 = relative_l2_difference(grid_p5.rho, grid_p7.rho)
    v_l2 = relative_l2_difference(grid_p5.v, grid_p7.v)

    # Normalize both fields before combining their squared differences so
    # neither density nor velocity dominates solely through its units.
    rho_scale = sqrt(sum(abs2, grid_p7.rho))
    v_scale = sqrt(sum(abs2, grid_p7.v))
    difference_measure =
        ((grid_p5.rho .- grid_p7.rho) ./
         max(rho_scale, eps(Float64))).^2 .+
        ((grid_p5.v .- grid_p7.v) ./
         max(v_scale, eps(Float64))).^2

    # This compact mask is zero in pure rho=1 and rho=2 fluid and maximal at
    # rho=1.5. It restricts the filament diagnostic to the mixing layer.
    mixing_weight = clamp.(4.0 .* (grid_p7.rho .- 1.0) .*
                           (2.0 .- grid_p7.rho), 0.0, 1.0)
    mixing_mask = mixing_weight .>= 0.25

    # Rolled-up filament candidates are the upper-vorticity quartile within
    # the density mixing layer, not a domain-wide velocity mask.
    vorticity = common_grid_vorticity(grid_p7)
    valid_mask = mixing_mask .& isfinite.(vorticity) .&
                 isfinite.(difference_measure)
    shear_values = vorticity[valid_mask]
    shear_threshold = isempty(shear_values) ? Inf :
                      quantile(shear_values, 0.75)
    filament_mask = valid_mask .& (vorticity .>= shear_threshold)

    finite_difference = isfinite.(difference_measure)
    total_difference = sum(difference_measure[finite_difference])
    filament_difference = sum(difference_measure[filament_mask])
    mixing_difference =
        sum((difference_measure .* mixing_weight)[finite_difference])

    if total_difference > 0.0
        filament_concentration = filament_difference / total_difference
        mixing_concentration = mixing_difference / total_difference
    else
        filament_concentration = 0.0
        mixing_concentration = 0.0
    end

    return (rho_l2=rho_l2,
            v_l2=v_l2,
            filament_concentration=filament_concentration,
            mixing_concentration=mixing_concentration,
            shear_threshold=shear_threshold)
end

# Start with the baseline primary archive. The late-time diagnostic below
# must be satisfied at both t=2 and t=3 before targeted AMR is activated.
npz_out_p7 = npz_out_p7_baseline
relative_difference_tolerance = 1.0e-6
filament_fraction_threshold = 0.5
mixing_layer_fraction_threshold = 0.5
late_time_diagnostics = NamedTuple[]

println("\nLate-time p=5/p=7 filament-difference trigger:")
println("  Filament mask: upper-quartile |vorticity| within the density mixing layer.")
for target_time in (2.0, 3.0)
    grid_p7_late = interpolate_amr_snapshot(
        snapshot_at_time(npz_out_p7_baseline, target_time))
    grid_p5_late = interpolate_amr_snapshot(
        snapshot_at_time(npz_out_p5, target_time))
    diagnostic = filament_difference_diagnostic(grid_p5_late, grid_p7_late)
    push!(late_time_diagnostics, diagnostic)

    Printf.@printf(
        "  t = %.1f: rho L2 = %.8e, v L2 = %.8e, filament fraction = %.6f, mixing-layer fraction = %.6f\n",
        target_time, diagnostic.rho_l2, diagnostic.v_l2,
        diagnostic.filament_concentration, diagnostic.mixing_concentration)
end

filament_triggered = all(
    diagnostic -> isfinite(diagnostic.rho_l2) &&
                  isfinite(diagnostic.v_l2) &&
                  max(diagnostic.rho_l2, diagnostic.v_l2) >
                      relative_difference_tolerance &&
                  diagnostic.filament_concentration >=
                      filament_fraction_threshold &&
                  diagnostic.mixing_concentration >=
                      mixing_layer_fraction_threshold,
    late_time_diagnostics)

println("Conditional shear-aware AMR activated: ", filament_triggered)
if filament_triggered
    println("Late-time differences persist in the filament mask; ",
            "running p=7 with mixing-layer shear-aware AMR and local max_level=7.")
    p7_targeted_run = run_targeted_p7()
    npz_out_p7 = p7_targeted_run.npz_directory

    # The targeted p=7 result becomes the primary run for subsequent output.
    sol_p7 = p7_targeted_run.sol
    semi_p7 = p7_targeted_run.semi
    ode_p7 = p7_targeted_run.ode
    checks_p7 = p7_targeted_run.checks
    primary_run_label = "p=7 (shear-aware targeted)"
else
    println("The filament trigger was not satisfied; ",
            "retaining baseline density AMR with max_level=6.")
    primary_run_label = "p=7"
end

println("Generating Rayleigh–Taylor summary plots...")
target_times = (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0)
primary_grids = [
    interpolate_amr_snapshot(snapshot_at_time(npz_out_p7, target_time))
    for target_time in target_times
]
```

Inspector summary:
 The implementation is a Trixi DGSEM solver for the 2D compressible Euler equations with a +y gravitational source. It uses polydeg=7 as the primary run, p=5 as a companion, and p=3 for the convergence trend. The domain is [0, 0.25] x [0, 1], t in [0, 3], gamma=1.4. Spatial discretization uses flux_hllc for surface fluxes and VolumeIntegralShockCapturingHG with IndicatorHennemannGassner on density, alpha_max=0.5, flux_ranocha, and flux_lax_friedrichs. AMR uses IndicatorLöhner on density with ControllerThreeLevel base=4, med=5, max=6, interval=20. Time integration uses RDPK3SpFSAL49 with StepsizeCallback and initial dt=1e-5. The initial density tanh profile, piecewise pressure, zero horizontal velocity, and single-mode vertical velocity perturbation are implemented as specified. The code saves NPZ archives every dt_save=0.1 and produces summary_all.png with rho and v at t=0.5, 1.0, 2.0, and 3.0.
Report: There are discrepancies with the base strategy:
The conditional targeted AMR enhancement does not implement the mandated shear/vorticity measure. The strategy requires that, if p=5/p=7 differences remain concentrated in the late-time filaments, the density AMR signal be augmented with a shear/vorticity measure restricted to the mixing layer. The code instead defines the conditional AMR variable as rho + 10*mixing_mask*vertical_velocity; it uses the vertical velocity amplitude itself, not a shear or vorticity measure. Although the density mask restricts this to the mixing layer and avoids global velocity refinement, it is a velocity-amplitude indicator rather than the specified shear/vorticity augmentation. The vorticity calculation in the code is used only for the filament-trigger diagnostic, not for the AMR variable.

  - [Planner]: Instructions received:
Cell 7: Dynamic Adaptive Mesh Refinement Strategy
- Action: Replace the conditional velocity-amplitude sensor with an actual vorticity sensor. Since `IndicatorLöhner`’s pointwise `variable(u, equations)` cannot evaluate spatial gradients, compute \(\omega=\partial v/\partial x-\partial u/\partial y\) on each DG element, mask it to the mixing layer, and apply the existing Löhner indicator to the augmented density signal. Baseline density AMR, controller thresholds, interval, and shock capturing remain unchanged.
- New Code (lines 6-56):
```julia
# The optional shear-aware configuration is activated only by the later
# late-time p=5/p=7 filament-difference trigger. It augments density with
# the magnitude of the DG vorticity, masked to the mixing layer, and permits
# one additional local refinement level, max_level=7. Pure light/heavy
# regions have zero vorticity-augmentation mask, preventing global refinement
# on velocity. Shock capturing is not changed.
# %%
@inline function mixing_layer_density_mask(rho)
    return clamp(4.0 * (rho - 1.0) * (2.0 - rho), 0.0, 1.0)
end

# IndicatorLöhner's pointwise variable API cannot evaluate velocity gradients.
# This wrapper computes vorticity on each DG element and then applies the
# original Löhner indicator to rho + shear_weight * mask * abs(vorticity).
struct MixingLayerVorticityIndicator{L}
    loehner::L
    shear_weight::Float64
end

function (indicator::MixingLayerVorticityIndicator)(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache)
    augmented_u = copy(u)
    n = size(u, 2)
    derivative_matrix = dg.basis.derivative_matrix
    node_coordinates = cache.elements.node_coordinates

    velocity_u = Matrix{Float64}(undef, n, n)
    velocity_v = Matrix{Float64}(undef, n, n)

    for element in axes(u, 4)
        # P4est elements in this setup are axis-aligned affine elements.
        # Convert derivatives from reference coordinates to physical coordinates.
        x_nodes = view(node_coordinates, 1, :, 1, element)
        y_nodes = view(node_coordinates, 2, 1, :, element)
        dx_dxi = 0.5 * (maximum(x_nodes) - minimum(x_nodes))
        dy_deta = 0.5 * (maximum(y_nodes) - minimum(y_nodes))

        for j in 1:n, i in 1:n
            rho = u[1, i, j, element]
            velocity_u[i, j] = u[2, i, j, element] / rho
            velocity_v[i, j] = u[3, i, j, element] / rho
        end

        for j in 1:n, i in 1:n
            dv_dx_reference = 0.0
            du_dy_reference = 0.0
            for k in 1:n
                dv_dx_reference += derivative_matrix[i, k] * velocity_v[k, j]
                du_dy_reference += derivative_matrix[j, k] * velocity_u[i, k]
            end

            # The 2D scalar vorticity is dv/dx - du/dy.
            vorticity = dv_dx_reference / dx_dxi -
                        du_dy_reference / dy_deta
            rho = u[1, i, j, element]
            augmented_u[1, i, j, element] =
                rho + indicator.shear_weight *
                mixing_layer_density_mask(rho) * abs(vorticity)
        end
    end

    # density_indicator reads the first component, so the existing Löhner
    # implementation now senses the density-plus-masked-vorticity signal.
    return indicator.loehner(augmented_u, mesh, equations, dg, cache)
end

function make_amr_callback(run_semi; shear_aware=false)
    density_loehner = IndicatorLöhner(run_semi, variable=density_indicator)

    if shear_aware
        indicator = MixingLayerVorticityIndicator(density_loehner, 10.0)
        max_level = 7
    else
        indicator = density_loehner
        max_level = 6
    end

    controller = ControllerThreeLevel(run_semi, indicator,
                                      base_level=4,
                                      med_level=5, med_threshold=0.1,
                                      max_level=max_level,
                                      max_threshold=0.3)
    callback = AMRCallback(run_semi, controller,
                           interval=20,
                           adapt_initial_condition=true)

    # Metadata allow archival and the conditional rerun to record the actual
    # AMR configuration rather than assuming that every run uses max_level=6.
    configuration = (indicator=indicator,
                     controller=controller,
                     shear_aware=shear_aware,
                     max_level=max_level,
                     levels=(4, 5, max_level),
                     interval=20)
    return callback, configuration
end
```

```julia
initial_dt = 1.0e-5
npz_out_p7_baseline = npz_out
npz_out_p5 = joinpath(npz_root, "p5")
npz_out_p3 = joinpath(npz_root, "p3")

# Check the fixed NPZ save schedule without loading the large state arrays.
# This permits reuse of complete Run #9 companion archives.
function npz_archive_has_all_save_times(directory)
    isdir(directory) || return false
    n_saves = round(Int, tspan[2] / dt_save)
    for save_index in 0:n_saves
        save_time = save_index * dt_save
        filename = joinpath(
            directory,
            Printf.@sprintf("time_%08.5f.npz", save_time))
        isfile(filename) || return false
    end
    return true
end

function solve_rti_ode(run_ode, run_callbacks)
    return solve(run_ode, RDPK3SpFSAL49();
                 dt=initial_dt,
                 save_everystep=false,
                 callback=run_callbacks)
end

# Construct and execute a run with exactly the primary run's numerics,
# changing only polydeg and, when requested, the conditional AMR indicator.
function run_rti_case(run_polydeg; shear_aware=false,
                      run_id="p$(run_polydeg)")
    run_basis = LobattoLegendreBasis(run_polydeg)
    run_indicator_sc = IndicatorHennemannGassner(
        equations, run_basis,
        alpha_max=0.5,
        alpha_min=0.001,
        alpha_smooth=true,
        variable=density_indicator)
    run_volume_integral = VolumeIntegralShockCapturingHG(
        run_indicator_sc;
        volume_flux_dg=flux_ranocha,
        volume_flux_fv=flux_lax_friedrichs)
    run_dg = DGSEM(polydeg=run_polydeg,
                   surface_flux=flux_hllc,
                   volume_integral=run_volume_integral)

    run_mesh = P4estMesh((1, 1), polydeg=run_polydeg,
                         coordinates_min=coordinates_min,
                         coordinates_max=coordinates_max,
                         periodicity=(true, false))
    run_semi = SemidiscretizationHyperbolic(
        run_mesh, equations, initial_condition_rti, run_dg;
        boundary_conditions=boundary_conditions,
        source_terms=source_terms_gravity)

    run_amr_callback, run_amr_configuration =
        make_amr_callback(run_semi; shear_aware=shear_aware)
    run_ode = semidiscretize(run_semi, tspan)
    run_callbacks = make_run_callbacks(
        run_semi, run_amr_callback, run_polydeg;
        run_amr_configuration=run_amr_configuration,
        run_id=run_id)

    println("Starting ", run_id,
            " with polydeg = ", run_polydeg,
            ", shear-aware AMR = ", shear_aware,
            ", initial dt = ", initial_dt)
    run_sol = solve_rti_ode(run_ode, run_callbacks)
    run_checks = (retcode=run_sol.retcode,
                  t_end=Float64(run_sol.t[end]))
    println("Finished ", run_id,
            " at t = ", run_checks.t_end,
            " with retcode ", run_checks.retcode)

    return (sol=run_sol,
            semi=run_semi,
            ode=run_ode,
            dg=run_dg,
            mesh=run_mesh,
            amr_configuration=run_amr_configuration,
            npz_directory=joinpath(npz_root, run_id),
            checks=run_checks)
end

# Execute the required new p=7 primary run using the objects constructed
# above, so its existing AMR, NPZ, restart, and stepsize callbacks are used.
println("Starting baseline p=7 primary run with initial dt = ", initial_dt)
sol = solve_rti_ode(ode, callbacks)
sol_p7 = sol
semi_p7 = semi
ode_p7 = ode
checks_p7 = (retcode=sol_p7.retcode,
             t_end=Float64(sol_p7.t[end]))
println("Finished baseline p=7 primary run at t = ", checks_p7.t_end,
        " with retcode ", checks_p7.retcode)

# Reuse a complete Run #9 p=5 archive; otherwise launch the required companion.
if npz_archive_has_all_save_times(npz_out_p5)
    println("Reusing complete archived p=5 companion snapshots in ",
            npz_out_p5)
    p5_run = nothing
else
    p5_run = run_rti_case(5; run_id="p5")
end

                        shear_aware=true,
                        run_id="p7_shear_aware")
end
```

```julia
# Construct this list only when called, after Cell 12 has selected either the
# baseline or conditionally targeted p=7 primary archive.
function current_archive_runs()
    return Tuple{String, String}[
        ("p=3", npz_out_p3),
        ("p=5", npz_out_p5),
        (primary_run_label, npz_out_p7)
    ]
end

function print_grid_metrics(label, grid)
    metrics = interface_metrics(grid)
    kinetic_energy = uniform_grid_kinetic_energy(grid)
    Printf.@printf(
        "  %s: bubble tip y = %.8f, spike tip y = %.8f, mixing width = %.8f, KE = %.12e\n",
        label, metrics.bubble_tip, metrics.spike_tip,
        metrics.mixing_width, kinetic_energy)
    return nothing
end

function log_archive_checks(label, directory)
    data = snapshot_at_time(directory, 3.0)
    final_time = snapshot_time(data)
    reached_t_end = isapprox(final_time, 3.0; atol=1.0e-10, rtol=0.0)
    u_cons = data["u_cons"]
    fields_finite = all(isfinite, u_cons)
    min_rho = Inf
    min_pressure = Inf
    gamma = equations.gamma

    for element in axes(u_cons, 4), j in axes(u_cons, 3), i in axes(u_cons, 2)
        rho    = u_cons[1, i, j, element]
        rho_v1 = u_cons[2, i, j, element]
        rho_v2 = u_cons[3, i, j, element]
        rho_e  = u_cons[4, i, j, element]
        pressure = (gamma - 1) *
                   (rho_e - 0.5 * (rho_v1^2 + rho_v2^2) / rho)
        min_rho = min(min_rho, rho)
        min_pressure = min(min_pressure, pressure)
    end

    println("[$label] successful arrival at t = 3.0: ", reached_t_end,
            " (final archived t = ", final_time, ")")
    println("[$label] all fields finite (no NaN/Inf): ", fields_finite)
    println("[$label] minimum rho = ", min_rho,
            "; min rho > 0: ", min_rho > 0)
    println("[$label] minimum p = ", min_pressure,
            "; min p > 0: ", min_pressure > 0)
    return nothing
end

# Called at the end of Cell 12, after all interpolation and diagnostic
# functions exist and the conditional targeted p=7 run has been resolved.
function run_certification_and_audit(primary_grids, target_times)
    archive_runs = current_archive_runs()

    println("Kinetic energy histories for the p=3/p=5/p=7 archives:")
    for (label, directory) in archive_runs
        times, energies = kinetic_energy_history(directory)
        println("  $label:")
        for (time, energy) in zip(times, energies)
            Printf.@printf("    t = %.2f, KE = %.12e\n", time, energy)
        end
    end

    println("\nCommon-grid polynomial-degree certification on the 512 x 2048 grid.")
    println("These are resolution-difference diagnostics, not errors against an exact solution.")
    println("The selected p=7 run is the comparison reference, not certified exact ground truth.")

    for target_time in (1.0, 2.0, 3.0)
        primary_index = findfirst(
            time -> isapprox(time, target_time; atol=1.0e-6, rtol=0.0),
            target_times)
        primary_index === nothing &&
            error("Primary p=7 grid is missing t = $target_time.")

        grid_p7 = primary_grids[primary_index]
        grid_p5 = interpolate_amr_snapshot(
            snapshot_at_time(npz_out_p5, target_time))
        grid_p3 = interpolate_amr_snapshot(
            snapshot_at_time(npz_out_p3, target_time))

        Printf.@printf("\nt = %.1f\n", target_time)
        print_grid_metrics("p=3", grid_p3)
        print_grid_metrics("p=5", grid_p5)
        print_grid_metrics(primary_run_label, grid_p7)

        l2_rho_p5 = relative_l2_difference(grid_p5.rho, grid_p7.rho)
        l2_v_p5 = relative_l2_difference(grid_p5.v, grid_p7.v)
        Printf.@printf(
            "  p=5 versus p=7 relative L2 resolution differences: rho = %.8e, v = %.8e\n",
            l2_rho_p5, l2_v_p5)

        l2_rho_p3 = relative_l2_difference(grid_p3.rho, grid_p7.rho)
        l2_v_p3 = relative_l2_difference(grid_p3.v, grid_p7.v)
        Printf.@printf(
            "  p=3 versus p=7 relative L2 resolution differences: rho = %.8e, v = %.8e\n",
            l2_rho_p3, l2_v_p3)
    end

    # Keep the integrity and positivity audit near the end of stdout.
    println("\nFinal integrity and positivity audit:")
    for (label, directory) in archive_runs
        log_archive_checks(label, directory)
    end
    println("Done.")
    return nothing
end
```

Inspector summary:
 The code implements the 2D compressible Euler equations with gravity source using Trixi.jl's DGSEM framework. The primary run uses polynomial degree p=7, with a p=5 companion and p=3 archive. Key numerical settings match the strategy: gamma=1.4, domain [0,0.25]x[0,1], t in [0,3], periodic x, slip walls at y=0 and y=1. Initial conditions include the tanh-smoothed density profile, piecewise pressure, and single-mode velocity perturbation. The gravitational source adds momentum and energy in the +y direction. Shock capturing uses IndicatorHennemannGassner on density with alpha_max=0.5, flux_hllc for surfaces, flux_ranocha and flux_lax_friedrichs for volume integrals. AMR uses IndicatorLöhner on density with ControllerThreeLevel (base=4, med=5, max=6) and interval=20. Time integration uses RDPK3SpFSAL49 with adaptive StepsizeCallback and initial dt=1e-5. The code includes a conditional shear-aware AMR augmentation with max_level=7 restricted to the mixing layer, triggered by late-time filament diagnostics. Data is archived every dt_save=0.1 as NPZ files containing the full conservative state and AMR metadata. Visualization produces summary_all.png with heatmaps of density and vertical velocity at t=0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0 from the primary run, with colorbars disabled and automatic scaling. The code also prints convergence metrics (L2 differences, spike/bubble tips, mixing width, kinetic energy histories) and performs a final integrity audit (t=3.0 reached, finiteness, min rho, min p).
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_PeriodicX_ReflectiveY_SmoothedDensityInterface\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

Create an appropiate Figure and saved it as summary_all.png

Debugger Status: REJECTED_FATAL
Debugger Analysis: The run fails in Cell 9 during initialization of the baseline p=7 solve: `solve_rti_ode` (script line 500), called at top level near line 566. The code adds a `StepsizeCallback` to both the primary `callbacks` and the callbacks returned by `make_run_callbacks`, but `solve_rti_ode` uses `RDPK3SpFSAL49` with its default adaptive time integration. Trixi explicitly rejects this combination: `ArgumentError: The StepsizeCallback has no effect when using an adaptive time integration scheme.` This is a fixable API-usage error, not an environment problem. Inspector summary:
 Numerical DGSEM implementation of the 2D compressible Euler equations with gravitational source. The code uses gamma=1.4, g=1.0, domain [0,0.25]x[0,1], t in [0,3], periodic x, and slip walls in y. The initial condition matches the strategy exactly: tanh-smoothed density interface at y=0.5, piecewise hydrostatic pressure, zero u, and v = -0.025 c(x,y) cos(8*pi*x) sin^6(pi*y). Spatial discretization is DGSEM with polydeg=7 primary, polydeg=5 companion, and p=3 fallback trend. It retains flux_hllc at surfaces, VolumeIntegralShockCapturingHG with IndicatorHennemannGassner on density and alpha_max=0.5, volume_flux_dg=flux_ranocha, and volume_flux_fv=flux_lax_friedrichs. AMR uses IndicatorLöhner on density with ControllerThreeLevel base=4, med=5, max=6, interval=20. The conditional shear-aware targeted AMR is implemented with a mixing-layer density mask and vorticity augmentation, permitting max_level=7 only when late-time p=5/p=7 filament diagnostics trigger. Time integration uses RDPK3SpFSAL49 with StepsizeCallback, initial dt=1e-5, and dt_save=0.1. The code saves full conservative state and AMR metadata every 0.1, produces summary_all.png with rho and v at t=0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0 using colorbar=false, and prints the required p=3/p=5/p=7 certification metrics and final finiteness/positivity audit.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_PeriodicX_ReflectiveY_SmoothedDensityInterface\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

**Output:**
Create an appropiate Figure and saved it as summary_all.png

Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_PeriodicX_ReflectiveY_SmoothedDensityInterface\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

## Section 1: Status Decision

**Status:** `REJECTED_FOR_REFINEMENT`

The simulation is stable and physically convincing, but the late-time solution is not yet certified as converged ground truth. Run 9 previously scored **95**, and the present Run 10 is only the **first subsequent refinement visible in the history**. Therefore, the five-post-success refinement saturation condition has not been reached.

## Section 2: Quantitative Scorecard

| **TOTAL SCORE** | **95 / 100** |

**Ground-truth error check:** No `Minimum achieved error:` line appears in the supplied log. **Quantitative error is not applicable as no ground truth is available.** The printed relative \(L_2\) values are differences between numerical resolutions, not errors against an exact solution.

## Section 3: Forensic Analysis & Feedback

### Observation

- **Integrity is excellent.** All three polynomial-degree runs reach \(t=3.0\), all archived fields are finite, and density and pressure remain strictly positive. For the primary \(p=7\) run, the minima are approximately \(\rho_{\min}=0.7706\) and \(p_{\min}=1.2136\). There is no evidence of NaN/Inf failure, positivity failure, or a blank/corrupted plot.
- **The qualitative physics is correct:**
  - At \(t=0.5\), the initially smoothed interface develops the expected single-mode deformation.
  - At \(t=1.0\), the central heavy-fluid spike rises while light fluid penetrates downward on either side.
  - At \(t=2.0\), a clear mushroom cap and symmetric roll-up structures have formed.
  - At \(t=3.0\), the spike approaches the upper wall and the density field contains elongated, thin Kelvin–Helmholtz-like filaments. The vertical-velocity panels localize the same plume and mixing-layer dynamics.
- The fields remain approximately symmetric about the domain centerline. There is no obvious periodic-seam artifact, wall penetration, or visible large-scale Gibbs ringing.
- The macroscopic \(p=7\) diagnostics are plausible: at \(t=3\), the bubble and spike tips are approximately \(y=0.1846\) and \(y=0.9286\), respectively, with mixing width \(0.7440\). Kinetic energy increases between the reported \(t=2\) and \(t=3\) states, consistent with gravitational potential-to-kinetic energy conversion.
- **The remaining concern is late-time convergence.** The \(p=5/p=7\) common-grid resolution differences are modest at \(t=2\)—about \(2.4\%\) for density and \(5.2\%\) for vertical velocity—but grow to approximately \(18.5\%\) and \(25.6\%\), respectively, at \(t=3\). Meanwhile, tip positions, mixing width, and kinetic energy are much closer. This combination indicates that the large-scale plume is converging substantially better than the small-scale filament locations and topology.

### Diagnosis

**Late-time shear/filament under-resolution and chaotic mixing sensitivity**, rather than a stability failure.

At late times, small differences in the rolled-up filaments can produce large pointwise \(L_2\) resolution differences even when the macroscopic plume diagnostics agree. Polynomial refinement alone cannot certify these structures: \(p=7\) is already at the recommended limit, and density-keyed refinement can under-prioritize shear-dominated regions once the interface becomes highly convoluted.

There is no visual justification for increasing shock-capturing dissipation or replacing HLLC with a more dissipative flux. Doing so would risk further smearing the contact and rolled-up filaments.

No exact-method override is warranted: the full nonlinear, multidimensional, gravitationally forced Euler solution with wall interactions and vortical mixing does not admit a usable closed-form or characteristic-based exact field solution through \(t=3\).

### Targeted Improvements

- **For Integrity:** **None.** Retain the adaptive time integration, current CFL control, HLLC surface flux, and positivity-preserving subcell stabilization.
- **For Accuracy:** Preserve the exact initial conditions, source term, and boundary conditions, but do not certify the archived fields as ground truth until the late-time filament sensitivity is reduced. Continue using tip positions, mixing width, kinetic energy, and common-grid field differences as **resolution diagnostics**, not exact-solution errors.
- **For Details:** Do **not** simply repeat the same \(p=7/p=5\) configuration.
  1. Keep `polydeg = 7`; do not increase it further.
  2. Strengthen the already-specified shear-aware AMR rather than merely leaving it conditional: activate it before or during mushroom roll-up, approximately \(t=1.5\)–\(2.0\), using vorticity or a velocity-gradient measure **restricted to the mixing layer**.
  3. Reduce the AMR interval from **20 to 10** and tighten the relevant refinement thresholds so thin filaments are refined before they roll up. Permit `max_level = 7` only in that local region.
  4. Use the archived \(p=7\) result as a baseline and perform a **\(p=7\) local-\(h\)-refinement comparison**. This isolates mesh sensitivity now that polynomial degree is capped. A further \(p=5\) companion is secondary and can be omitted if cost is limiting.
  5. Retain `flux_hllc`, `flux_ranocha`, and `alpha_max = 0.5`; there is no observed ringing warranting additional dissipation. Avoid global refinement or global velocity-based AMR.
- **For Optimality:** **None.** DGSEM with contact-resolving HLLC flux, entropy-stable DG volume flux, subcell shock capturing, and targeted AMR remains an appropriate high-fidelity method for this problem.

--- 🔄 Strategy Iteration 1/5 ---
--- Strategy Report Received ---
# Scientific Strategy Report — 2D Compressible Euler Rayleigh–Taylor Instability

**1. Problem Statement**

Heavy fluid ($\rho=2$) rests below light fluid ($\rho=1$) with gravity acting in $+y$ — i.e., gravity points from the heavy into the light fluid. This is the classically unstable Rayleigh–Taylor configuration. A single-mode vertical velocity perturbation seeds the interface, which grows through linear amplification → nonlinear spike/bubble formation → mushroom-cap roll-up with Kelvin–Helmholtz vortices → a late-time chaotic mixing layer that interacts with the slip walls at $y=0,1$. The flow is inviscid, so there is no physical mechanism to arrest small-scale generation: thin filaments and shear layers persist to $t=3.0$.

---

**1. Mathematical Specification (Code-Ready Extraction)**

* **Governing Equations:** 2D Compressible Euler with gravitational source, conservative variables $U = (\rho,\ \rho u,\ \rho v,\ E)^T$:

$$\partial_t \rho + \partial_x(\rho u) + \partial_y(\rho v) = 0$$
$$\partial_t(\rho u) + \partial_x(\rho u^2 + p) + \partial_y(\rho u v) = 0$$
$$\partial_t(\rho v) + \partial_x(\rho u v) + \partial_y(\rho v^2 + p) = \rho g$$
$$\partial_t E + \partial_x\big(u(E+p)\big) + \partial_y\big(v(E+p)\big) = \rho g\, v$$

with total energy $E = \dfrac{p}{\gamma-1} + \dfrac{1}{2}\rho(u^2+v^2)$, $\gamma = 1.4$, and $\mathbf{g} = (0,\ g)^T$, $g = 1.0$ (momentum source in $+y$; energy source is the gravitational work $\rho v g$).

* **Domain:** $(x,y) \in [0,\ 0.25] \times [0,\ 1.0]$, $\quad t \in [0,\ 3.0]$.

* **Boundary Conditions:**
  * $x = 0$ and $x = 0.25$: **Periodic**.
  * $y = 0$ and $y = 1$: **Reflective / free-slip walls** (no-penetration $v = 0$; mirror the normal velocity component).

* **Initial Conditions** (interface at $y_0 = 0.5$; implement exactly):
  * Density:
    $$\rho(x,y,0) = 2.0 - \frac{1}{2}\Big(1 + \tanh\big(50\,(y - 0.5)\big)\Big)$$
  * Horizontal velocity: $u(x,y,0) = 0$
  * Pressure (piecewise hydrostatic, continuous at $y=0.5$ with $p=2.0$):
    $$p(y,0) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1, & y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5, & y \geq 0.5 \end{cases}$$
  * Local sound speed: $c(y) = \sqrt{\gamma\, p(y)\, /\, \rho(y)}$
  * Vertical velocity perturbation (single mode, wavelength $\lambda_x = 0.25$):
    $$v(x,y,0) = -0.025\; c(y)\, \cos(8\pi x)\, \sin^6(\pi y)$$
  * Total energy:
    $$E(x,y,0) = \frac{p(y)}{\gamma - 1} + \frac{1}{2}\,\rho(y)\, v^2(x,y,0)$$

* **Constants & Parameters:** $\gamma = 1.4$; $g = 1.0$ with $\mathbf{g} = [0,\ 1]^T$; $\rho_{heavy}=2.0$, $\rho_{light}=1.0$; interface smoothing parameter $50$; perturbation amplitude $0.025$; wavenumber $k_x = 8\pi$; Atwood number $A = 1/3$; $\mu = 0$, $k_{\text{thermal}} = 0$.

* **Expected Physics:**
  * $t \approx 0.5$: single-mode deformation of the smoothed interface.
  * $t \approx 1.0$: central heavy-fluid spike rises; light fluid penetrates downward on either side.
  * $t \approx 1.5$–$2.0$: mushroom cap and symmetric Kelvin–Helmholtz roll-up (onset of shear-layer-dominated dynamics).
  * $t \approx 3.0$: spike tip approaches the upper wall ($y \approx 0.93$), bubble tip near $y \approx 0.18$, mixing width $\approx 0.74$, elongated thin filaments. Positivity of $\rho$, $p$ must be preserved near walls and in low-density plume cores.

---

**2. Strategic Methodology**

* **Can we obtain an exact solution?** **No.** The full nonlinear, multidimensional, gravitationally forced Euler system with non-trivial initial data, wall reflections, and vortex roll-up admits no closed-form solution. The Method of Characteristics applies only to 1D simple-wave problems; in 2D the characteristics form caustics immediately and the solution becomes genuinely multidimensional and chaotic (RTI mixing). No separation-of-variables, Green's function, or similarity reduction is available. The Advisor explicitly confirms no exact-method override is warranted.
* **Method Selection:** **Discontinuous Galerkin Spectral Element Method in 2D plus time.** Justification: (i) local $h$/$p$ refinement is essential to track the convoluted interface and thin filaments; (ii) subcell shock capturing guarantees positivity near walls and at contact steepening; (iii) entropy-stable DG volume fluxes handle the strong shear layers. Fourier spectral is ruled out: the domain is non-periodic in $y$, the initial pressure has a kink at $y=0.5$, and late-time filaments would cause Gibbs contamination.

---

**3. Implementation Requirements (CRITICAL)**

* **Configuration:** Use template defaults for the base mesh and time integration (retain the adaptive CFL-controlled Runge–Kutta stepping of prior runs), **except** the Advisor-mandated overrides in 3.1.

* **3.0.1 Adaptive Mesh Refinement & Shock Capturing — Variable Selection Analysis:**
  * *Structural features:* the material interface (contact discontinuity) and the rolled-up shear layers. *Chaotic variables:* the velocity field, which becomes globally turbulent inside the mixing layer at late times.
  * **Primary AMR / shock-capturing variable: Density $\rho$.** It cleanly and persistently marks the interface and filaments — sharp only at the features of interest, bounded, and never globally chaotic. It is superior to velocity (globally noisy → wasted elements; Advisor explicitly forbids global velocity-based AMR) and to pressure (smooth, hydrostatic-dominated, a poor interface marker).
  * **Secondary shear-aware indicator: vorticity** $\omega = \partial_x v - \partial_y u$ (or velocity-gradient norm). Vorticity is generated at the KH-unstable interface and remains localized to the mixing layer even when velocity is globally noisy. It **must be masked to the mixing layer** (e.g., cells with intermediate density $1.1 \lesssim \rho \lesssimim 1.9$) so the chaotic exterior cannot trigger refinement. This directly addresses the Advisor's diagnosis that density-keyed refinement alone under-prioritizes shear-dominated regions once the interface is highly convoluted.
  * Shock capturing: Hennemann–Gassner indicator keyed on $\rho$ (positivity-preserving subcell blending), retained unchanged.

* **3.1 Advisor-Mandated Adjustments (Overrides — supersede template defaults):**
  1. **`polydeg = 7`** for the primary run. Do not increase further.
  2. **AMR interval reduced from 20 to 10**; tighten the Löhner refinement thresholds so thin filaments are refined *before* they roll up.
  3. **Shear-aware AMR strengthened from conditional to active:** activate the vorticity/velocity-gradient indicator (masked to the mixing layer) at $t \approx 1.5$, before/during mushroom roll-up; permit `max_level = 7` **only** in that local region (global density-keyed AMR remains capped at its standard controller levels).
  4. **Convergence study — p=7 local-h-refinement comparison:** use the archived p=7 result as baseline and run a companion p=7 case with the AMR controller shifted one level finer (isolating mesh sensitivity at fixed polynomial degree). The p=5 companion is secondary and may be omitted if cost-limited.
  5. **Retain unchanged:** `flux_hllc` (surface), `flux_ranocha` (DG volume), Lax–Friedrichs FV subcell flux, `alpha_max = 0.5`, adaptive time integration with current CFL control. **No additional dissipation** — no ringing was observed, and extra dissipation would smear the contact and filaments.
  6. **Forbidden:** global refinement, global velocity-based AMR, more dissipative surface fluxes.

---

**4. Visualization Strategy**

* **Field Selection (Analysis):** Density $\rho$ is the primary driver — buoyancy is proportional to $\rho$, and it captures the interface, spike/bubble plumes, and mixing layer. Vertical velocity $v = \rho v / \rho$ is the second field — it localizes the plume ascent/descent and the shear-layer roll-up. Do not plot $u$, $p$, or $E$ (subplots would become unreadable).
* **Task:** Plot $\rho$ and $v$ at **4 time steps: $t = 0.5,\ 1.0,\ 2.0,\ 3.0$**.
* **Layout:** Grid — 2 rows (fields: $\rho$, $v$) × 4 columns (times).
* **Saving Data:** Archive the **full conservative state** (all variables) every `dt_save = 0.1` for ground-truth archiving; plotting selection affects only the summary image.
* **Constraints:**
  * Save as: `summary_all.png` (strictly).
  * **CRITICAL: Disable colorbars (`colorbar = false`)** — the code is in Julia/Trixi.
  * No fixed colormaps; let scales adjust automatically.
  * Plot only primary variables; no derived quantities (no vorticity panels) unless the Advisor requests them.

---

**5. Cross-Validation & Data Integration (Conditional)**

* **Trigger check:** The [USER_QUESTION] contains **no external data paths** (no sensors, no reference solution). No external interpolation or pointwise comparison is required.
* **Internal convergence diagnostics (Advisor-mandated):** Compute and print, between the p=7 baseline and the p=7 h-refined companion on a common uniform grid at $t=2$ and $t=3$: relative $L_2$ differences of $\rho$ and $v$, bubble/spike tip positions, mixing width, and kinetic-energy history. **These are resolution diagnostics, not exact-solution errors** — no ground truth exists for this configuration, and the archived fields must not be certified as such until late-time filament sensitivity is reduced.

- New Code (lines 1-104):
```julia
# ## Methodology: Feature-Driven $h$-Refinement
# Density remains the primary AMR variable. The density controller uses
# interval=10 and tightened thresholds so filaments are refined before roll-up.
#
# Shear-aware refinement is armed at t=1.5 by the activation callback returned
# in the configuration. Until then, only density AMR is active. Once armed,
# masked DG vorticity can raise mixing-layer elements to level 7. Density-keyed
# refinement outside the mixing layer remains capped at level 6.
#
# level_shift=1 provides the fixed-p=7 local-h-refinement comparison by shifting
# the feature-targeted medium/maximum levels, without raising the global base
# level. Shock capturing is not changed.
# %%
@inline function mixing_layer_density_mask(rho)
    return clamp(4.0 * (rho - 1.0) * (2.0 - rho), 0.0, 1.0)
end

# Restrict level-7 refinement to elements containing intermediate-density fluid.
@inline function mixing_layer_element(u, element)
    for j in axes(u, 3), i in axes(u, 2)
        rho = u[1, i, j, element]
        if 1.1 <= rho <= 1.9
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

# IndicatorLöhner's pointwise variable API cannot evaluate velocity gradients.
# This wrapper computes vorticity on each DG element and then applies the
# original Löhner indicator to rho + shear_weight * mask * abs(vorticity).
struct MixingLayerVorticityIndicator{L}
    loehner::L
    shear_weight::Float64
end

function (indicator::MixingLayerVorticityIndicator)(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache)
    augmented_u = copy(u)
    n = size(u, 2)
    derivative_matrix = dg.basis.derivative_matrix
    node_coordinates = cache.elements.node_coordinates

    velocity_u = Matrix{Float64}(undef, n, n)
    velocity_v = Matrix{Float64}(undef, n, n)

    for element in axes(u, 4)
        # P4est elements in this setup are axis-aligned affine elements.
        # Convert derivatives from reference coordinates to physical coordinates.
        x_nodes = view(node_coordinates, 1, :, 1, element)
        y_nodes = view(node_coordinates, 2, 1, :, element)
        dx_dxi = 0.5 * (maximum(x_nodes) - minimum(x_nodes))
        dy_deta = 0.5 * (maximum(y_nodes) - minimum(y_nodes))

        for j in 1:n, i in 1:n
            rho = u[1, i, j, element]
            velocity_u[i, j] = u[2, i, j, element] / rho
            velocity_v[i, j] = u[3, i, j, element] / rho
        end

        for j in 1:n, i in 1:n
            dv_dx_reference = 0.0
            du_dy_reference = 0.0
            for k in 1:n
                dv_dx_reference += derivative_matrix[i, k] * velocity_v[k, j]
                du_dy_reference += derivative_matrix[j, k] * velocity_u[i, k]
            end

            # The 2D scalar vorticity is dv/dx - du/dy.
            vorticity = dv_dx_reference / dx_dxi -
                        du_dy_reference / dy_deta
            rho = u[1, i, j, element]
            augmented_u[1, i, j, element] =
                rho + indicator.shear_weight *
                mixing_layer_density_mask(rho) * abs(vorticity)
        end
    end

    return indicator.loehner(augmented_u, mesh, equations, dg, cache)
end

# Compose the standard density controller with a locally restricted shear
# controller. ControllerThreeLevel returns the target level of each leaf element.
struct LocalShearAMRController{D, S}
    density_controller::D
    shear_indicator::S
    shear_active::Base.RefValue{Bool}
    shear_threshold::Float64
    density_max_level::Int
    shear_max_level::Int
end

function (controller::LocalShearAMRController)(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache)
    lambda = copy(controller.density_controller(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache))
    mixing_elements = BitVector(undef, length(lambda))

    for element in eachindex(lambda)
        mixing_elements[element] = mixing_layer_element(u, element)

        # A level-shifted companion may request level 7 from density, but never
        # outside the mixing layer. The standard global density cap remains 6.
        if !mixing_elements[element]
            lambda[element] = min(lambda[element],
                                  controller.density_max_level)
        end
    end

    # Before t=1.5 this branch is disabled, so no velocity-gradient-based AMR
    # is performed. After activation, only masked mixing-layer elements can
    # receive the additional local refinement level.
    if controller.shear_active[] && controller.shear_indicator !== nothing
        alpha = controller.shear_indicator(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache)
        for element in eachindex(lambda)
            if mixing_elements[element] &&
               alpha[element] > controller.shear_threshold
                lambda[element] = max(lambda[element],
                                      controller.shear_max_level)
            end
        end
    end

    return lambda
end

function make_amr_callback(run_semi; shear_aware=true, level_shift=0)
    level_shift in (0, 1) ||
        throw(ArgumentError("level_shift must be either 0 or 1"))

    density_loehner = IndicatorLöhner(run_semi, variable=density_indicator)

    # Keep base_level=4 to avoid global refinement. For the companion, shift
    # only the feature-targeted medium and maximum density levels.
    base_level = 4
    med_level = 5 + level_shift
    density_max_level = 6 + level_shift

    density_controller = ControllerThreeLevel(
        run_semi, density_loehner,
        base_level=base_level,
        med_level=med_level, med_threshold=0.05,
        max_level=density_max_level, max_threshold=0.15)

    shear_active = Ref(false)
    if shear_aware
        shear_loehner = IndicatorLöhner(run_semi, variable=density_indicator)
        shear_indicator = MixingLayerVorticityIndicator(shear_loehner, 10.0)
    else
        shear_indicator = nothing
    end

    controller = LocalShearAMRController(
        density_controller, shear_indicator, shear_active,
        0.15, 6, 7)

    callback = AMRCallback(run_semi, controller,
                           interval=10,
                           adapt_initial_condition=true)

    # Cell 9 registers this callback before the AMR callback. At t=1.5 it arms
    # shear refinement; the next AMR invocation can then apply local level 7.
    shear_start_time = 1.5
    function activate_shear_amr(integrator)
        shear_active[] = shear_aware
        return nothing
    end
    activation_callback = PresetTimeCallback(
        [shear_start_time], activate_shear_amr;
        save_positions=(false, false))

    max_level = (shear_aware || level_shift == 1) ? 7 : 6
    indicator = shear_aware ? shear_indicator : density_loehner

    configuration = (indicator=indicator,
                     controller=controller,
                     shear_aware=shear_aware,
                     max_level=max_level,
                     levels=(base_level, med_level, max_level),
                     interval=10,
                     activation_callback=activation_callback,
                     shear_start_time=shear_start_time,
                     level_shift=level_shift)
    return callback, configuration
end

# Strengthened p=7 configuration: density AMR from t=0 and active,
# mixing-layer-restricted shear AMR from t=1.5.
amr_callback, amr_configuration = make_amr_callback(
    semi; shear_aware=true, level_shift=0)
amr_indicator = amr_configuration.indicator
amr_controller = amr_configuration.controller
```

```julia
# Construct otherwise identical callbacks for companion runs. The activation
# callback must precede AMR so shear refinement is armed at t=1.5 before the
# next mesh-adaptation interval.
function make_run_callbacks(run_semi, run_amr_callback, run_polydeg;
                            run_amr_configuration=(shear_aware=false,
                                                   max_level=6,
                                                   levels=(4, 5, 6),
                                                   interval=10,
                                                   activation_callback=nothing,
                                                   shear_start_time=Inf,
                                                   level_shift=0),
                            run_id="p$(run_polydeg)")
    run_npz_out = joinpath(npz_root, run_id)
    run_save_npz_callback, run_save_final_npz_callback =
        make_npz_callbacks(run_npz_out, run_polydeg;
                           run_amr_configuration=run_amr_configuration)

    run_trixi_out = joinpath(trixi_root, run_id)
    run_save_solution = SaveSolutionCallback(
        dt=dt_save,
        save_initial_solution=true,
        save_final_solution=true,
        output_directory=run_trixi_out,
        solution_variables=cons2cons)
    run_stepsize_callback = StepsizeCallback(cfl=0.5)

    run_activation_callback = nothing
    if hasproperty(run_amr_configuration, :activation_callback)
        run_activation_callback =
            run_amr_configuration.activation_callback
    end

    amr_callbacks = if run_activation_callback === nothing
        (run_amr_callback,)
    else
        (run_activation_callback, run_amr_callback)
    end

    return CallbackSet(SummaryCallback(),
                       AnalysisCallback(run_semi,
                                        interval=analysis_interval),
                       AliveCallback(analysis_interval=analysis_interval),
                       run_save_solution,
                       amr_callbacks...,
                       run_stepsize_callback,
                       run_save_npz_callback,
                       run_save_final_npz_callback)
end

initial_dt = 1.0e-5

# npz_out is the existing p=7 archive created by earlier runs. The new
# companion uses a different directory and cannot overwrite that baseline.
npz_out_p7_baseline = npz_out
npz_out_p7_h_refined = joinpath(npz_root, "p7_h_refined")
npz_out_p7 = npz_out_p7_h_refined
primary_run_label = "p=7 (shear-aware, local h-refined)"

# Check the fixed NPZ save schedule without loading the large state arrays.
function npz_archive_has_all_save_times(directory)
    isdir(directory) || return false
    n_saves = round(Int, tspan[2] / dt_save)
    for save_index in 0:n_saves
        save_time = save_index * dt_save
        filename = joinpath(
            directory,
            Printf.@sprintf("time_%08.5f.npz", save_time))
        isfile(filename) || return false
    end
    return true
end

function solve_rti_ode(run_ode, run_callbacks)
    return solve(run_ode, RDPK3SpFSAL49();
                 adaptive=false,
                 dt=initial_dt,
                 save_everystep=false,
                 callback=run_callbacks)
end

# Construct and execute a run with identical equations, fluxes, shock
# capturing, mesh topology, and time integration. Only the requested AMR
# configuration and output identifier change.
function run_rti_case(run_polydeg; shear_aware=false, level_shift=0,
                      run_id="p$(run_polydeg)")
    run_basis = LobattoLegendreBasis(run_polydeg)
    run_indicator_sc = IndicatorHennemannGassner(
        equations, run_basis,
        alpha_max=0.5,
        alpha_min=0.001,
        alpha_smooth=true,
        variable=density_indicator)
    run_volume_integral = VolumeIntegralShockCapturingHG(
        run_indicator_sc;
        volume_flux_dg=flux_ranocha,
        volume_flux_fv=flux_lax_friedrichs)
    run_dg = DGSEM(polydeg=run_polydeg,
                   surface_flux=flux_hllc,
                   volume_integral=run_volume_integral)
    run_mesh = P4estMesh((1, 1), polydeg=run_polydeg,
                         coordinates_min=coordinates_min,
                         coordinates_max=coordinates_max,
                         periodicity=(true, false))
    run_semi = SemidiscretizationHyperbolic(
        run_mesh, equations, initial_condition_rti, run_dg;
        boundary_conditions=boundary_conditions,
        source_terms=source_terms_gravity)

    run_amr_callback, run_amr_configuration =
        make_amr_callback(run_semi;
                          shear_aware=shear_aware,
                          level_shift=level_shift)
    run_ode = semidiscretize(run_semi, tspan)
    run_callbacks = make_run_callbacks(
        run_semi, run_amr_callback, run_polydeg;
        run_amr_configuration=run_amr_configuration,
        run_id=run_id)

    println("Starting ", run_id,
            " with polydeg = ", run_polydeg,
            ", shear-aware AMR = ", shear_aware,
            ", controller level shift = ", level_shift,
            ", AMR interval = ", run_amr_configuration.interval,
            ", initial dt = ", initial_dt)
    run_sol = solve_rti_ode(run_ode, run_callbacks)
    run_checks = (retcode=run_sol.retcode,
                  t_end=Float64(run_sol.t[end]))
    println("Finished ", run_id,
            " at t = ", run_checks.t_end,
            " with retcode ", run_checks.retcode)

    return (sol=run_sol,
            semi=run_semi,
            ode=run_ode,
            dg=run_dg,
            mesh=run_mesh,
            amr_configuration=run_amr_configuration,
            npz_directory=joinpath(npz_root, run_id),
            checks=run_checks)
end

# Reuse the archived p=7 result as the resolution baseline. If it is unavailable,
# generate a strengthened level_shift=0 baseline once so that the comparison
# remains self-contained. No p=3 or p=5 run is required for this refinement.
p7_baseline_run = nothing
if npz_archive_has_all_save_times(npz_out_p7_baseline)
    println("Reusing complete archived p=7 baseline snapshots in ",
            npz_out_p7_baseline)

        shear_aware=true,
        level_shift=0,
        run_id="p7_strengthened_baseline")
    npz_out_p7_baseline = p7_baseline_run.npz_directory
end

npz_archive_has_all_save_times(npz_out_p7_baseline) ||
    error("The p=7 baseline NPZ archive is incomplete; " *
```

- New Code (lines 75-132):
```julia
# Called at the end of Cell 12 after all interpolation and diagnostic
# functions exist. The legacy function name is retained for call compatibility,
# but this routine performs resolution diagnostics, not exact-solution certification.
function run_certification_and_audit(primary_grids, target_times)
    archive_runs = current_archive_runs()

    println("Kinetic energy histories for the fixed-p=7 baseline " *
            "and local-h-refined companion:")
    for (label, directory) in archive_runs
        times, energies = kinetic_energy_history(directory)
        println("  $label:")
        for (time, energy) in zip(times, energies)
            Printf.@printf("    t = %.2f, KE = %.12e\n", time, energy)
        end
    end

    println("\nCommon-grid fixed-p=7 local-h-refinement comparison " *
            "on the 512 x 2048 grid.")
    println("These are resolution-difference diagnostics, " *
            "not errors against an exact solution.")
    println("The p=7 baseline is only a comparison reference; " *
            "neither archive is certified exact ground truth.")

    for target_time in (2.0, 3.0)
        primary_index = findfirst(
            time -> isapprox(time, target_time; atol=1.0e-6, rtol=0.0),
            target_times)
        primary_index === nothing &&
            error("Primary p=7 grid is missing t = $target_time.")

        # Reuse the already interpolated companion grid and interpolate the
        # baseline onto exactly the same cell-centered uniform grid.
        grid_h_refined = primary_grids[primary_index]
        grid_baseline = interpolate_amr_snapshot(
            snapshot_at_time(npz_out_p7_baseline, target_time))

        Printf.@printf("\nt = %.1f\n", target_time)
        print_grid_metrics("p=7 baseline", grid_baseline)
        print_grid_metrics(primary_run_label, grid_h_refined)

        # Normalize by the baseline field. These values measure sensitivity
        # between resolutions and must not be reported as ground-truth errors.
        l2_rho = relative_l2_difference(grid_h_refined.rho,
                                        grid_baseline.rho)
        l2_v = relative_l2_difference(grid_h_refined.v,
                                      grid_baseline.v)
        Printf.@printf(
            "  p=7 h-refined versus baseline relative L2 resolution differences: rho = %.8e, v = %.8e\n",
            l2_rho, l2_v)
    end

    # Keep the integrity and positivity audit near the end of stdout.
    println("\nFinal integrity and positivity audit:")
    for (label, directory) in archive_runs
        log_archive_checks(label, directory)
    end
    println("Done.")
    return nothing
end
```

Inspector summary:
 The code implements a 2D compressible Euler Rayleigh–Taylor simulation using Trixi DGSEM with polydeg=7, HLLC surface flux, Ranocha DG volume flux, Lax–Friedrichs FV subcell flux, Hennemann–Gassner density-keyed shock capturing with alpha_max=0.5, periodic x-boundaries and slip walls in y, domain [0,0.25]×[0,1], tspan (0,3.0), gamma=1.4, and gravity source terms (0, 0, rho*g, g*rho_v2). The initial condition matches the strategy: rho = 2 − 0.5(1 + tanh(50(y−0.5))), piecewise hydrostatic pressure p=2y+1 for y<0.5 and p=y+1.5 for y>=0.5, u=0, and v=−0.025 c(y) cos(8πx) sin^6(πy). AMR uses interval=10 with a density-keyed Löhner controller, plus a vorticity-based shear indicator masked to 1.1<=rho<=1.9, activated at t=1.5, with local max_level=7 and non-mixing-density elements capped at level 6. A p=7 baseline (archived/reused or fallback run) and a p=7 h-refined companion with level_shift=1 are run. Post-processing prints common-grid resolution diagnostics at t=2 and t=3: relative L2 differences of rho and v, bubble/spike tip positions, mixing width, and kinetic-energy histories. It also audits finiteness, positivity of rho and p, and successful arrival at t=3.0.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_PeriodicX_ReflectiveY_SmoothedDensityInterface\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

The required shear-aware AMR is not actually activated: make_amr_callback defines a shear_active Ref(false) and a custom vorticity controller, but no activation callback is created to set it true at t approximately 1.5, and the function does not return the (AMRCallback, configuration) tuple that run_rti_case destructures. The main callbacks block also references undefined amr_callback and amr_configuration, and make_npz_callbacks is called with an undefined amr_configuration. Thus the mandated local level-7 mixing-layer-restricted AMR at t approximately 1.5 is missing and the main execution path is inconsistent. Additionally, the P4estMesh constructor omits the commented initial_refinement_level=3, so the base mesh may not match the intended template default.

```julia
                     interval=10,
```

Inspector summary:
 The code implements the 2D compressible Euler Rayleigh–Taylor problem with gamma=1.4 and gravity in +y via source terms (0, 0, rho*g, rho*v*g). The domain is [0,0.25] x [0,1] for t in [0,3], with periodic x-boundaries and slip walls at y=0,1. Initial conditions match the strategy: rho = 2 - 0.5*(1+tanh(50*(y-0.5))), piecewise hydrostatic pressure, v = -0.025*c(y)*cos(8*pi*x)*sin(pi*y)^6, and total energy via prim2cons. The spatial method is DGSEM with polydeg=7, HLLC surface flux, Ranocha DG volume flux, Lax–Friedrichs FV subcell flux, and Hennemann–Gassner density-keyed shock capturing with alpha_max=0.5. AMR uses interval=10, a density Löhner controller, and mixing-layer-masked vorticity shear refinement activated at t=1.5, with max_level=7 restricted to the mixing layer. The code runs a p=7 baseline and a p=7 h-refined companion, computes common-grid relative L2 differences for rho and v at t=2 and t=3, bubble/spike tips, mixing width, and kinetic-energy histories. Visualization plots rho and v at t=0.5,1.0,2.0,3.0 in a 2x4 grid saved as summary_all.png with colorbar=false. Time integration uses RDPK3SpFSAL49 with StepsizeCallback cfl=0.5.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_PeriodicX_ReflectiveY_SmoothedDensityInterface\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

New Code (lines 84-110):
```julia
# Forward Trixi's element-variable query to the supported density controller.
# This allows SaveSolutionCallback to serialize AMR metadata without requiring
# Trixi to have specialized support for this custom controller wrapper.
function Trixi.get_element_variables!(element_variables, u, mesh, equations, dg,
                                      cache,
                                      controller::LocalShearAMRController)
    Trixi.get_element_variables!(element_variables, u, mesh, equations, dg, cache,
                                 controller.density_controller)
    return element_variables
end

function (controller::LocalShearAMRController)(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache)
    lambda = copy(controller.density_controller(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache))
    mixing_elements = BitVector(undef, length(lambda))
    for element in eachindex(lambda)
        mixing_elements[element] = mixing_layer_element(u, element)
        # A level-shifted companion may request level 7 from density, but never
        # outside the mixing layer. The standard global density cap remains 6.
        if !mixing_elements[element]
            lambda[element] = min(lambda[element],
                                  controller.density_max_level)
        end
    end
    # Before t=1.5 this branch is disabled, so no velocity-gradient-based AMR
    # is performed. After activation, only masked mixing-layer elements can
    # receive the additional local refinement level.
    if controller.shear_active[] && controller.shear_indicator !== nothing
        alpha = controller.shear_indicator(u, mesh, equations, dg, cache)
        for element in eachindex(lambda)
            if mixing_elements[element] &&
               alpha[element] > controller.shear_threshold
                lambda[element] = max(lambda[element],
                                      controller.shear_max_level)
            end
        end
    end
    return lambda
end
```

Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_PeriodicX_ReflectiveY_SmoothedDensityInterface\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.
